# Previous defaults vs Recover probe + one-packet probe + probe timer + Cruise queue 0.5 BDP (depth 4, 7 links x 100 RTTs)

Utilization = delivered / sum of the true C (`util10`: from RTT 10 on).  `maxq` = mean belief `max_q` in BDPs (true C).  Step time over the RTTs where the search ran.

## Belief per RTT

C, B: the true link.  rate: what the CCA chose; sent: what the application let it send; deliv, loss: delivered and newly lost this RTT.  Beliefs are `[min, max]` as the CCA chose the rate (from the observations up to that RTT); K: the burstiness hypotheses the belief assumes.
